    11	impl Parser {
    12	    pub(super) fn parse_require(&mut self) -> Fallible<Stmt> {
    13	        let site = self.take("require")?.site;
    14	        let condition = self.expression(0, &["else"])?;
    15	        // @ref LLP 3000.036#14.5 — name an unsupported text-pattern
    16	        // operator before diagnosing a refusal code the author already wrote.
    17	        let pattern = match &condition {
    18	            Expr::ShapeUse { base, name, site }
    19	                if super::TEXT_PATTERN_WORDS.contains(&name.to_ascii_lowercase().as_str()) =>
    20	            {
    21	                Some((name.clone(), print::expression(base), site.clone()))
    22	            }
    23	            _ => {
    24	                let next = self.peek();
    25	                (next.kind == Kind::Word
    26	                    && super::TEXT_PATTERN_WORDS.contains(&next.value.to_ascii_lowercase().as_str()))
    27	                .then(|| (next.value.clone(), print::expression(&condition), next.site.clone()))
    28	            }
    29	        };
    30	        if let Some((word, operand, at)) = pattern {
    31	            return Err(syntax_with(
    32	                &at,
    33	                format!("Quarry has no `{word}` operator; `{operand} {word} …` is not a condition"),
    34	                format!("compare whole text with `=` or `!=`; bound its length in the type (`{operand}: text 3..80`); declare a link `text <=200 format url` (http or https; anything else refuses E_INPUT before the program runs) or a handle `format handle`; or check the pattern in the client"),
    35	            )
    36	            .guide("types"));
    37	        }
    38	        // @ref LLP 3000.036.002#9 — a multiline require refusal preserves the author's code in its repair.
    39	        if self.at_kind(Kind::Newline) {
    40	            let mut at = 1;
    41	            while matches!(self.peek_at(at).kind, Kind::Newline | Kind::Indent | Kind::Dedent) {
    42	                at += 1;
    43	            }
    44	            if self.peek_at(at).value == "else" {
    45	                let code = self.peek_at(at + 1).value.clone();
    46	                return Err(syntax_with(
    47	                    &site,
    48	                    "a require's else must be on the condition's closing line",
    49	                    format!("put `else {code}` on the closing line: `require {} else {code}`", print::expression(&condition)),
    50	                )
    51	                .guide("programs"));
    52	            }
    53	        }
    54	        if !self.at("else") {
    55	            return Err(syntax_with(
    56	                &site,
    57	                "require needs a refusal code: require <condition> else CODE",
    58	                format!("require {} else NOT_ALLOWED", print::expression(&condition)),
    59	            ));
    60	        }
    61	        self.take("else")?;
    62	        let code = self.word()?.value;
    63	        let message = if self.at_kind(Kind::Str) { Some(self.take_kind(Kind::Str)?.value) } else { None };
    64	        self.newline()?;
    65	        Ok(Stmt::Require { condition, code, message, site })
    66	    }
    67	
    68	    /// `schedule name every <duration> [at HH:MM]: job`, at least one minute,
    69	    /// or `every N month[s]|year[s] [at [day D] HH:MM]`.
    70	    pub(super) fn parse_schedule(&mut self) -> Fallible<ScheduleDecl> {
    71	        let site = self.take("schedule")?.site;
    72	        let rewrite = "schedule digest every 1d at 08:00: sendDigests";
    73	        let name = self.binding_name()?;
    74	        if !self.maybe("every") {
    75	            return Err(syntax_with(&site, "a schedule fires every so often: schedule name every 2m: job", rewrite));
    76	        }
    77	        let amount = self.take_kind(Kind::Number)?;
    78	        let unit = self.word()?;
    79	        // @ref LLP 3000.040#24-monthly-and-yearly-schedules — calendar months and years, UTC, anchored at 1970-01.
    80	        let calendar = match unit.value.as_str() { "month" | "months" => Some(1), "year" | "years" => Some(12), _ => None };
    81	        if let Some(per) = calendar {
    82	            let rewrite = "schedule billing every 1 month at day 1 09:00: bill";
    83	            let months = amount.value.parse::<u32>().ok().filter(|n| *n >= 1).and_then(|n| n.checked_mul(per))
    84	                .filter(|n| *n <= snapback4_core::schema::CALENDAR_MONTHS_LIMIT)
    85	                .ok_or_else(|| syntax_with(&amount.site, "a calendar schedule fires every 1 to 1200 months (100 years)", rewrite).guide("jobs"))?;
    86	            let mut offset_ms = 0;
    87	            if self.maybe("at") {
    88	                let mut day = 1;
    89	                if self.maybe("day") {
    90	                    let token = self.peek().clone();
    91	                    day = self.number()?;
    92	                    if !(1..=28).contains(&day) {
    93	                        return Err(syntax_with(&token.site, format!("day {day} is not in every month; a calendar schedule's day is 1 through 28"),
    94	                            "at day 28 09:00, or a daily schedule whose job tests month(periodStart) != month(periodEnd): that daily period crosses a month's end").guide("jobs"));
    95	                    }

use super::*;

#[test]
fn intentional_input_cases_and_later_operations_are_not_fixture_drift() {
    let p = Project::new();
    p.source("use personas alice\nquery answer(n: int 1..5):\n  return n\n");
    fs::create_dir(p.0.join("fixtures")).unwrap();
    let cases = [
        r#"{"op":"answer","args":{"n":6},"expect":"E_INPUT"}"#,
        r#"{"op":"answer","args":{},"expect":"E_INPUT"}"#,
        r#"{"op":"answer","args":{"n":"wrong"},"expect":"E_INPUT"}"#,
        r#"{"op":"answer","args":{"n":2,"extra":1},"expect":"E_INPUT"}"#,
        r#"{"op":"answer","args":false,"expect":"E_INPUT"}"#,
        r#"{"op":"answer","args":{"n":6},"expect":"denied"}"#,
        r#"{"op":"answer","args":{"n":6},"expect":"refused"}"#,
        r#"{"op":"answer","args":{"n":6},"expect":"failed"}"#,
        r#"{"op":"answer","args":{"n":6},"expect":"NOT_ALLOWED"}"#,
    ].join("\n");
    fs::write(p.0.join("fixtures/test.cases.jsonl"), &cases).unwrap();
    let out = p.run(&["check", "--json"]);
    assert!(out.status.success(), "{out:?}");
    assert!(!parsed(&out)["notes"].as_array().unwrap().iter().any(|n| n["code"] == "N_FIXTURE_ARGUMENT_DRIFT"));
    let test = p.run(&["test", "--fixture", "fixtures"]);
    assert!(test.status.success(), "{test:?}");
    fs::write(p.0.join("fixtures/test.cases.jsonl"), format!("{cases}\n{{\"op\":\"later\",\"args\":{{}}}}\n{{\"op\":\"answer\",\"args\":{{\"n\":6}},\"expect\":\"sent\"}}\n")).unwrap();
    let out = p.run(&["check", "--json"]);
    let json = parsed(&out);
    let notes: Vec<_> = json["notes"].as_array().unwrap().iter().filter(|n| n["code"] == "N_FIXTURE_ARGUMENT_DRIFT").collect();
    assert_eq!(notes.len(), 1, "{json}");
    let test = p.run(&["test", "--fixture", "fixtures"]);
    assert!(!test.status.success());
    let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&test.stderr);
    assert!(text.contains("unknown operation later"), "{test:?}");
    assert!(!p.store().exists());
}

#[test]
fn production_readiness_groups_dependents_and_collapses_persona_identity() {
    let p = Project::new();
    let source = "use identity password\ntable staff:\n  principal: principal\n  unique principal\n  read <- .principal = viewer\n  insert <- exists staff[viewer]\n  online only\ntable rooms:\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- exists staff[viewer]\n  online only\ntable bookings:\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- exists staff[viewer]\n  online only\nmutation staffer(principal: principal):\n  return insert staff { principal }\nmutation room():\n  return insert rooms {}\nmutation booking():\n  return insert bookings {}\n";
    p.source(source);
    let out = p.run(&["check", "--json"]);
    assert!(out.status.success(), "{out:?}");
    let json = parsed(&out);
    let notes: Vec<_> = json["notes"].as_array().unwrap().iter().filter(|n| n["code"] == "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER").collect();
    assert_eq!(notes.len(), 1, "{json}");
    let msg = notes[0]["message"].as_str().unwrap();
    assert!(msg.starts_with("staff:") && msg.contains("bookings, rooms depend on it"), "{msg}");
    p.source(&source.replace("use identity password", "use personas alice"));
    let out = p.run(&["check", "--json"]);
    let json = parsed(&out);
    let notes: Vec<_> = json["notes"].as_array().unwrap().iter().filter(|n| n["code"] == "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER").collect();
    assert_eq!(notes.len(), 1, "{json}");
    assert!(notes[0]["message"].as_str().unwrap().starts_with("persona-only: safe to ignore without `use identity`; no production identity"));
    for seat in ["ops", "not a seat", ""] {
        p.source(&source.replace("insert <- exists staff[viewer]", &format!("insert <- viewer = 'service:{seat}' or exists staff[viewer]")));
        let out = p.run(&["check", "--json"]); assert!(out.status.success(), "{out:?}");
        let json = parsed(&out);
        let count = json["notes"].as_array().unwrap().iter().filter(|n| n["code"] == "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER").count();
        assert_eq!(count, usize::from(seat != "ops"), "{seat}: {json}");
    }
}

#[test]
fn fixture_constraints_keep_the_column_and_code_instead_of_not_found() {
    let p = Project::new();
    p.source("table proofs:\n  version: int 1..\n  label: text <=30\n  unique label else DUPLICATE\n  public 'proofs'\nquery versions():\n  return proofs first 10 by .id\n");
    fs::create_dir(p.0.join("fixtures")).unwrap();
    for (rows, expected) in [
        (r#"{"id":"p","version":0,"label":"a"}"#, "proofs.version"),
        ("{\"id\":\"p1\",\"version\":1,\"label\":\"a\"}\n{\"id\":\"p2\",\"version\":1,\"label\":\"a\"}", "DUPLICATE"),
    ] {
        fs::write(p.0.join("fixtures/proofs.jsonl"), rows).unwrap();
        let out = p.run(&["test", "--fixture", "fixtures"]);
        assert!(!out.status.success());
        let text = format!("{}{}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout), String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr));
        assert!(text.contains(expected) && !text.contains("NOT_FOUND"), "{text}");
        assert!(text.contains("E_CONSTRAINT") || text.contains("DUPLICATE"), "{text}");
    }
    assert!(!p.store().exists());
}

 succeeded in 0ms:
//! `test.cases.jsonl` beside the fixture tables: the application's own
//! operations, in order, as named viewers, each with the outcome it expects.
//! One line is `{"as": "alice", "op": "addCard", "args": {..}, "expect": "sent"}`;
//! `expect` is `sent` (a mutation's default), `complete`, `capped`, `ok` (any
//! answer), or a refusal code (`E_RULE`, `NOT_FOUND`, a program's own `require`
//! code). A query's default is its `check` verdict: `complete`, or either
//! answer when `check` notes the query is never complete (N_NEVER_COMPLETE).
//! Optional `data` must equal the answer; `count` is the answer's length; `save`
//! names the answer so a later string argument `"$name"` or `"$name.field"`
//! reuses it. `as` is a declared persona, as on dev: an undeclared one is
//! refused E_AUTH.
use crate::project::persona_principal;
use snapback4_core::engine::refusal_trace::RefusalTrace;
use snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind;
use snapback4_core::{Context, Engine, Refusal, WriteState, wire};
use snapback4_lang::Bound;
use serde_json::{Value as Json, json};
pub fn run(engine: &mut Engine, bound: &Bound, fixture: &str, sources: &[(String, String)]) -> Result<(usize, usize), String> {
    let path = Path::new(fixture).join(FILE);
    let text = match std::fs::read_to_string(&path) {
        Ok(text) => text,
        Err(error) if error.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Ok((0, 0)),
        Err(error) => return Err(format!("cannot read {}: {error}", path.display())),
    };
    let mut saved = BTreeMap::<String, Json>::new();
    let (mut passed, mut failed) = (0, 0);
    let mut verdicts_shown = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
    let lines: Vec<(usize, &str)> = text.lines().enumerate().filter(|(_, line)| !line.trim().is_empty() && !line.trim_start().starts_with("//")).collect();
    // Every line is checked before any runs: a misspelled key must not pass
    // silently (wave 5: `"rows":0` passed against one row), and a typo on the
    // last line should not wait for every earlier case.
    let mut cases = Vec::with_capacity(lines.len());
    for (index, line) in &lines {
        let at = format!("{FILE}:{}", index + 1);
        let case: Json = serde_json::from_str(line).map_err(|error| format!("{at}: {error}"))?;
        validate(&case).map_err(|why| format!("{at}: {why}"))?;
        cases.push((at, case));
    }
    println!("test: cases from {FILE}");
    for (at, case) in cases {
        let Some(op) = case.get("op").and_then(Json::as_str) else { return Err(format!("{at}: each case names \"op\" (a query or mutation)")) };
        let Some(bound_program) = bound.programs.iter().find(|p| p.program.name == op) else {
            let starter = super::init::starter_fixture(FILE).is_some_and(|starter| starter == text);
            let hint = if starter { format!("\n  hint: {FILE} is the todo starter's unmodified cases for list/add/toggle/remove; `snapback4 init --adapt` removes it (write your own cases, `snapback4 guide testing`)") } else { String::new() };
            return Err(format!("{at}: unknown operation {op}{hint}"));
        };
        let program = bound_program.program.clone();
        let persona = case.get("as").and_then(Json::as_str).unwrap_or_else(|| default_persona(&bound.personas));
        let principal = if persona.contains(':') { persona.to_owned() } else { persona_principal(persona) };
        // A query's default expectation is check's verdict: a noted never-complete page passes capped.
        let never_complete = (program.kind == ProgramKind::Query).then(|| never_complete(bound, bound_program)).flatten();
        let explicit = case.get("expect").and_then(Json::as_str);
        let expect = explicit.unwrap_or(match program.kind { ProgramKind::Query if never_complete.is_some() => "ok", ProgramKind::Query => "complete", _ => "sent" });
        let label = case.get("label").and_then(Json::as_str).map(|l| format!(" — {l}")).unwrap_or_default();
        let raw_args = substitute(case.get("args").cloned().unwrap_or_else(|| json!({})), &saved).map_err(|why| format!("{at}: {why}"))?;
        let input = (|| -> Result<_, Refusal> {
            if let Some(why)=crate::persona_principals::arguments(&bound.personas,&program.args,&raw_args) { return Err(why); }
            let mut args = BTreeMap::new();
            let fields = raw_args.as_object().ok_or_else(|| Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "args must be an object"))?;
            for (name, value) in fields {
                let kind = program.args.get(name).ok_or_else(|| Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("{op} takes no argument {name}")))?;
                args.insert(name.clone(), wire::from_json(kind, value).map_err(|why| Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("argument {name}: {why}")))?);
            }
            Ok(args)
        })();
        let input_refusal = input.as_ref().err().cloned();
        let args = input.unwrap_or_default();
        let trace = RefusalTrace::default();
        let context_viewer = principal.clone();
        let context = Context::new(principal, args).with_refusal_trace(trace.clone());
        // (outcome word, answer) or a refusal
        let mut unavailable = false;
        let got: Result<(&str, Json), Refusal> = if let Some(refusal) = input_refusal { Err(refusal) }
        else if let Some(why) = undeclared_persona(persona, &bound.personas) {
            Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", why))
        } else { match program.kind {
            ProgramKind::Query => match engine.query(op, context) {
                Ok(outcome) => { unavailable = outcome.unavailable; Ok((if outcome.complete { "complete" } else { "capped" }, wire::to_json(&outcome.data))) },
                Err(refusal) => Err(refusal),
            },
            ProgramKind::Job => return Err(format!("{at}: {op} is a job; cases run queries and mutations")),
            _ => match engine.mutate(op, None, context) {
                Ok(outcome) => match outcome.state {
                    WriteState::Sent { result, .. } => Ok(("sent", wire::to_json(&result))),
                    WriteState::Failed { why, .. } => Err(why),
                },
                Err(error) => Err(error.refusal()),
            },
        } };
        let mut problems = Vec::new();
        let mut notes = Vec::new();
        let shown = match &got {
            Ok((word, answer)) => {
                let admitted = *word == expect || expect == "ok" || (expect == "complete" && *word == "sent");
                if !admitted { problems.push(if expect == "capped" && *word == "complete" {
                    "now complete — update the case: replace expect: \"capped\" with expect: \"complete\" (or omit expect); readable pinned-parent guards can now certify this page".to_owned()
                if let Some(name) = case.get("save").and_then(Json::as_str) { saved.insert(name.to_owned(), answer.clone()); }
                (*word).to_owned()
            }
            Err(refusal) => {
                let code = &refusal.code;
                if code != expect && !(expect == "denied" || expect == "refused" || expect == "failed") {
                    problems.push(format!("expected {expect}, got {code}: {}", refusal.message));
                    notes.extend(refusal_evidence(&trace, refusal, bound, sources, explicit, &context_viewer));
                }
                code.clone()
            }
        };
        if problems.is_empty() {
            passed += 1;
            println!("      ok   {at} {op} ({persona}) → {shown}{label}");
            if let Err(refusal) = &got && refusal.code == "E_INPUT" { println!("           {}", refusal.message); }
        } else {
            failed += 1;
            println!("      FAIL {at} {op} ({persona}) → {shown}{label}: {}", problems.join("; "));
        }
        if program.kind == ProgramKind::Query && trace.hidden() > 0 { println!("           hidden: {} (encountered row images filtered by read rules)", trace.hidden()); }
        for note in notes { println!("           {note}"); }
    }
    Ok((passed, failed))
}

/// The keys a case line may carry.
pub const KEYS: [&str; 8] = ["as", "op", "args", "expect", "data", "count", "save", "label"];

// @ref LLP 3000.036#15.3 — an unknown key is refused, never ignored: ignoring it was a false green.
fn validate(case: &Json) -> Result<(), String> {
    let Json::Object(fields) = case else { return Err("each case is a JSON object: {\"op\":..,\"as\":..,\"args\":{..},\"expect\":..}".into()) };
    let valid = KEYS.iter().map(|key| format!("`{key}`")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
    for key in fields.keys() {
        if KEYS.contains(&key.as_str()) { continue; }
        let meant = match key.to_ascii_lowercase().as_str() {
            "rows" | "length" | "len" | "size" | "n" | "total" | "counts" => Some("count"),
            "arguments" | "params" | "parameters" | "input" | "inputs" | "arg" => Some("args"),
            "persona" | "viewer" | "user" | "who" | "principal" => Some("as"),
            "operation" | "query" | "mutation" | "name" => Some("op"),
            "result" | "answer" | "value" | "rowsequal" | "equals" => Some("data"),
            "expected" | "expects" | "outcome" | "state" | "code" | "status" => Some("expect"),
            "description" | "desc" | "comment" | "note" | "title" => Some("label"),
            _ => None,
        };
        let hint = meant.map(|key| format!(" (did you mean `{key}`?)")).unwrap_or_default();
        return Err(format!("unknown key `{key}`{hint}; a case takes only {valid} (snapback4 guide testing)"));
    }
    for key in ["as", "op", "expect", "save", "label"] {
        if let Some(value) = fields.get(key) && !value.is_string() {
            let hint = if key == "expect" && value.is_object() { "; compare the answer with `data` (the whole answer) or `count` (its length) beside `expect`" } else { "" };
            return Err(format!("`{key}` is a string, got {value}{hint}"));
        }
    }
    if let Some(name) = fields.get("save").and_then(Json::as_str) && (name.is_empty() || name.contains('.') || name.starts_with('$')) {
        return Err(format!("save alias {name:?} must be nonempty with no dot or leading dollar; use a name such as r1, then reference $r1 or $r1.field"));
    }
    if let Some(value) = fields.get("count") && value.as_u64().is_none() { return Err(format!("`count` is a non-negative integer, got {value}")); }
    if let Some(value) = fields.get("args") && !value.is_object() && fields.get("expect").and_then(Json::as_str) != Some("E_INPUT") { return Err("\"args\" is an object".into()); }
    Ok(())
}

   105	fn strip_parens(text: &str) -> String {
   106	    let trimmed = text.trim();
   107	    if trimmed.starts_with('(') && trimmed.ends_with(')') {
   108	        // Only strip a pair that encloses the whole text.

   114	        return trimmed[1..trimmed.len() - 1].to_owned();
   115	    }
   116	    trimmed.to_owned()
   117	}

use snapback4_lang::{compile, diag::SourceRewriteKind, Diagnostic};
use snapback4_core::{Context, Engine, Value, WriteState, value::object};

fn refusal(source: &str, code: &str) -> Diagnostic {
    let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
    bound.diagnostics.iter().find(|diagnostic| diagnostic.code == code).cloned()
        .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("missing {code}; diagnostics: {:#?}", bound.diagnostics))
}

fn emitted_source_compiles(source: &str, authored: &str, diagnostic: &Diagnostic) -> String {
    let emitted = diagnostic.source_rewrite.as_ref().expect("a verified source rewrite");
    let repaired = source.replacen(authored, emitted, 1);
    assert_ne!(repaired, source, "fixture must contain the authored source");
    let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), repaired.clone())]);
    assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "emitted rewrite did not compile:\n{emitted}\n{:#?}", bound.diagnostics);
    repaired
}

fn classified(diagnostic: &Diagnostic, expected: SourceRewriteKind) {
    let actual = diagnostic.source_rewrite_kind.as_ref().expect("a concrete rewrite category");
    // This deliberately stays exhaustive: a new concrete rule-edit category
    // makes the golden fail to compile until it has a fixture and semantics case.
    match actual {
        SourceRewriteKind::MixedKey => assert_eq!(expected, SourceRewriteKind::MixedKey),
        SourceRewriteKind::MembershipBootstrap => assert_eq!(expected, SourceRewriteKind::MembershipBootstrap),
        SourceRewriteKind::WriteResultNarrowing => assert_eq!(expected, SourceRewriteKind::WriteResultNarrowing),
        SourceRewriteKind::NamedUpdate => assert_eq!(expected, SourceRewriteKind::NamedUpdate),
    }
}

#[test]
fn every_concrete_rule_rewrite_is_the_emitted_text_and_compiles_in_its_schema() {
    let positional = "table roles:\n  principal: principal\n  role: enum('staff', 'member')\n  unique principal, role\n  public 'roles'\ntable records:\n  owner: principal\n  read <- exists roles[viewer, role = 'staff']\n  online only\nquery visible():\n  return records first 10 by .id\n";
    let diagnostic = refusal(positional, "E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN");
    classified(&diagnostic, SourceRewriteKind::MixedKey);
    assert_eq!(diagnostic.source_rewrite.as_deref(), Some("roles[principal = viewer, role = 'staff']"));
    emitted_source_compiles(positional, "roles[viewer, role = 'staff']", &diagnostic);

    let update = "table fees:\n  status: enum('pending', 'confirmed')\n  public 'fees'\n  update <- old.status = 'pending' and next.status = 'confirmed'\n  online only\nmutation transition(fee: fees, status: enum('pending', 'confirmed')):\n  update fees[fee] { status }\n  return null\n";
    let diagnostic = refusal(update, "E_QUARRY_RULE_OPERATOR");
    classified(&diagnostic, SourceRewriteKind::NamedUpdate);
    assert_eq!(diagnostic.source_rewrite.as_deref(), Some("update (old, next) <- (old.status = 'pending' and next.status = 'confirmed')"));
    emitted_source_compiles(update, "update <- old.status = 'pending' and next.status = 'confirmed'", &diagnostic);

    let partially_named = "table fees:\n  owner: principal\n  status: enum('pending', 'archived')\n  public 'fees'\n  update <- .owner = viewer and old.status != 'archived'\n  online only\n";
    let diagnostic = refusal(partially_named, "E_QUARRY_RULE_OPERATOR");
    classified(&diagnostic, SourceRewriteKind::NamedUpdate);
    assert_eq!(
        diagnostic.source_rewrite.as_deref(),
        Some("update (old, next) <- ((next.owner = viewer) and (old.status != 'archived'))")
    );
    let repaired = emitted_source_compiles(
        partially_named,
        "update <- .owner = viewer and old.status != 'archived'",
        &diagnostic,
    );
    assert!(repaired.contains("update (old, next)") && repaired.contains("next.owner"));
}

#[test]
fn emitted_source_rewrites_match_independent_reference_semantics() {
    let positional = "table roles:\n  principal: principal\n  role: enum('staff', 'member')\n  unique principal, role\n  public 'roles'\ntable records:\n  owner: principal\n  read <- exists roles[viewer, role = 'staff']\n  online only\nquery visible():\n  return records first 10 by .id\n";
    let diagnostic = refusal(positional, "E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN");
    let repaired = emitted_source_compiles(positional, "roles[viewer, role = 'staff']", &diagnostic);
    // Execute the actual compiler-emitted replacement over the independent
    // population-membership truth table.
    for has_pair in [false, true] {
        let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), repaired.clone())]);
        let mut engine = Engine::memory(bound.schema, bound.programs.into_iter().map(|program| program.program)).unwrap();
        let mut rows = vec![("records".into(), object([("id", Value::Id("record".into())), ("owner", Value::Id("owner".into()))]))];
        if has_pair {
            rows.push(("roles".into(), object([("id", Value::Id("role".into())), ("principal", Value::Id("alice".into())), ("role", Value::String("staff".into()))])));
        }
        engine.seed(rows).unwrap();
        let read = engine.query("visible", Context::new("alice", Default::default())).unwrap();
        let Value::Array(rows) = read.data else { panic!("expected page, got {:?}", read.data) };
        assert_eq!(!rows.is_empty(), has_pair);
    }

    let update = "table fees:\n  status: enum('pending', 'confirmed')\n  public 'fees'\n  update <- old.status = 'pending' and next.status = 'confirmed'\n  online only\nmutation transition(fee: fees, status: enum('pending', 'confirmed')):\n  update fees[fee] { status }\n  return null\n";
    let diagnostic = refusal(update, "E_QUARRY_RULE_OPERATOR");
    let repaired = emitted_source_compiles(update, "update <- old.status = 'pending' and next.status = 'confirmed'", &diagnostic);
    // Execute the actual named-update line for every old/next status pair.
    for old in ["pending", "confirmed"] {
        for next in ["pending", "confirmed"] {
            let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), repaired.clone())]);
            let mut engine = Engine::memory(bound.schema, bound.programs.into_iter().map(|program| program.program)).unwrap();
            engine.seed([("fees".into(), object([("id", Value::Id("fee".into())), ("status", Value::String(old.into()))]))]).unwrap();
            let outcome = engine.mutate("transition", None, Context::new("alice", [
                ("fee".into(), Value::Id("fee".into())), ("status".into(), Value::String(next.into())),
            ].into())).unwrap();
            let allowed = matches!(outcome.state, WriteState::Sent { .. });
            let old_pending = old == "pending";
            let next_confirmed = next == "confirmed";
            assert_eq!(allowed, old_pending && next_confirmed, "{old} -> {next}: {outcome:?}");
        }
    }

    let parents = "table scenes:\n  revision: int\n  public 'scenes'\ntable boundaries:\n  revision: int\n  public 'boundaries'\ntable instructions:\n  scene: scenes\n  boundary: boundaries\n  revision: int\n  read <- .boundary.revision = .scene.revision\n  online only\nquery visible():\n  return instructions first 10 by .id\n";
    let diagnostic = refusal(parents, "E_QUARRY_RULE_HOP");
    let predicate = diagnostic.rewrite.split('`').skip(1).step_by(2)
        .find(|piece| piece.starts_with("exists boundaries["))
        .expect("the emitted stored-field predicate");
    let repaired = parents.replacen(".boundary.revision = .scene.revision", predicate, 1);
    let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), repaired.clone())]);
    assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "emitted stored-field predicate did not compile:\n{predicate}\n{:#?}", bound.diagnostics);
    // Execute the actual emitted predicate under the invariant named by the
    // guidance: the local revision equals the boundary revision at insert.
    for boundary in [1, 2] {
        for scene in [1, 2] {
            let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), repaired.clone())]);
            let mut engine = Engine::memory(bound.schema, bound.programs.into_iter().map(|program| program.program)).unwrap();
            engine.seed([
                ("scenes".into(), object([("id", Value::Id("scene".into())), ("revision", Value::Int(scene))])),
                ("boundaries".into(), object([("id", Value::Id("boundary".into())), ("revision", Value::Int(boundary))])),
                ("instructions".into(), object([
                    ("id", Value::Id("instruction".into())), ("scene", Value::Id("scene".into())),
                    ("boundary", Value::Id("boundary".into())), ("revision", Value::Int(boundary)),
                ])),
            ]).unwrap();
            let read = engine.query("visible", Context::new("alice", Default::default())).unwrap();
            let Value::Array(rows) = read.data else { panic!("expected page, got {:?}", read.data) };
            assert_eq!(!rows.is_empty(), boundary == scene, "boundary={boundary}, scene={scene}");
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn design_guidance_is_not_mislabeled_as_a_source_rewrite() {
    let expiry = "table hearings:\n  releaseAt: time\n  public 'hearings'\ntable notices:\n  hearing: hearings\n  read <- now >= .hearing.releaseAt\n  online only\n";
    let diagnostic = refusal(expiry, "E_QUARRY_RULE_OPERATOR");
    assert!(diagnostic.source_rewrite.is_none());
    assert!(!diagnostic.rewrite.contains("expire at .hearing"), "{diagnostic}");
    assert!(diagnostic.rewrite.contains("required time column") && diagnostic.rewrite.contains("not a reference hop"), "{diagnostic}");

    let parents = "table scenes:\n  revision: int\n  public 'scenes'\ntable boundaries:\n  revision: int\n  public 'boundaries'\ntable instructions:\n  scene: scenes\n  boundary: boundaries\n  revision: int\n  read <- .boundary.revision = .scene.revision\n  online only\n";
    let diagnostic = refusal(parents, "E_QUARRY_RULE_HOP");
    assert!(diagnostic.source_rewrite.is_none());
    assert!(diagnostic.rewrite.contains("stored-field invariant") && diagnostic.rewrite.contains("exists boundaries[id = .boundary, revision = .revision]")
        && diagnostic.rewrite.contains("exists scenes[id = .scene, revision = .revision]"), "{diagnostic}");
    assert!(!diagnostic.rewrite.contains("revision = exists"), "{diagnostic}");

    let operands = "table payments:\n  firstApprover: principal ?\n  public 'payments'\ntable ledger:\n  payment: payments\n  firstApprover: principal\n  insert <- .firstApprover = .payment.firstApprover\n  online only\n";
    let diagnostic = refusal(operands, "E_QUARRY_TYPE");
    assert!(diagnostic.source_rewrite.is_none());
    assert!(diagnostic.message.contains("principal") && diagnostic.message.contains("principal ?"), "{diagnostic}");
    assert!(!diagnostic.message.contains("viewer meets"), "{diagnostic}");
}

#[test]
fn nonunique_point_advice_distinguishes_rules_from_programs() {
    let schema = "table roles:\n  principal: principal\n  role: enum('staff', 'member')\n  by byPrincipal: principal\n  public 'roles'\n";
    let rule = format!("{schema}table records:\n  owner: principal\n  read <- exists roles[principal = viewer] by byPrincipal\n  online only\n");
    let diagnostic = refusal(&rule, "E_QUARRY_POINT_KEY");
    assert!(diagnostic.rewrite.contains("table rules cannot scan a population"), "{diagnostic}");
    assert!(!diagnostic.rewrite.contains("where … first N"), "{diagnostic}");

    let program = format!("{schema}query role(who: principal):\n  return exists roles[principal = who] by byPrincipal\n");
    let diagnostic = refusal(&program, "E_QUARRY_POINT_KEY");
    assert!(diagnostic.rewrite.contains("where … first N"), "{diagnostic}");
    assert!(!diagnostic.rewrite.contains("table rules cannot"), "{diagnostic}");
}

#[test]
fn wave10_mixed_extra_tests_and_nullable_upsert_rewrites_compile() {
    let schema = "table trades:\n  public 'trades'\ntable receipts:\n  trade: trades\n  side: enum('offer', 'want')\n  received: bool\n  unique trade, side\n  public 'receipts'\n";
    for tail in ["query received(trade: trades):\n  return exists receipts[trade, side = 'offer', received = true]\n", "table records:\n  trade: trades\n  read <- exists receipts[.trade, side = 'offer', received = true]\n  online only\n"] {
        let source = format!("{schema}{tail}");
        let d = refusal(&source, "E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN");
        classified(&d, SourceRewriteKind::MixedKey);
        let old = if tail.starts_with("query") { "receipts[trade, side = 'offer', received = true]" } else { "receipts[.trade, side = 'offer', received = true]" };
        emitted_source_compiles(&source, old, &d);
        assert!(d.source_rewrite.unwrap().contains("trade = "));
    }
    let source = "table props:\n  label: text <=30\n  unique label\n  public 'props'\ntable uses:\n  prop: props\n  public 'uses'\nmutation add(label: text <=30):\n  p = upsert props[label = label] { label }\n  return insert uses { prop: p.id }\n";
    // DX3 proves a public, ordinary-lifetime upsert result required. QDIAG
    // also accepts that row directly in its declared reference destination.
    for accepted in [source.to_owned(), source.replace("prop: p.id", "prop: p")] {
        let b = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), accepted)]);
        assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", b.diagnostics);
    }
    // Keep the actual nullable-authority repair controls independently.
    let source = source.replace("public 'props'", "read <- .label = 'visible'\n  insert <- allow\n  update <- allow");
    let source = source.as_str();
    let d = refusal(source, "E_QUARRY_TYPE");
    classified(&d, SourceRewriteKind::WriteResultNarrowing);
    assert!(d.message.contains("upsert") && d.message.contains("unreadable") && d.rewrite.contains("rolls back"), "{d}");
    emitted_source_compiles(source, "p.id", &d);
    let row_source = source.replace("prop: p.id", "prop: p");
    let d = refusal(&row_source, "E_QUARRY_TYPE");
    classified(&d, SourceRewriteKind::WriteResultNarrowing);
    let repaired = row_source.replace("{ prop: p }", &format!("{{ prop: {} }}", d.source_rewrite.as_ref().unwrap()));
    let b = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), repaired)]);
    assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", b.diagnostics);
}

#[test]
fn wave10_emitted_service_bootstrap_compiles_and_has_no_tautology() {
    let source = "table staff:\n  principal: principal\n  unique principal\n  immutable principal\n  read <- exists staff[viewer]\n  insert <- .principal = viewer or exists staff[viewer]\n  sync\ntable records:\n  read <- exists staff[viewer]\n  sync\nmutation grant(principal: principal):\n  return insert staff { principal }\n";
    let d = refusal(source, "E_SYNC");
    classified(&d, SourceRewriteKind::MembershipBootstrap);
    assert!(d.rewrite.starts_with("insert <- exists staff[viewer];"), "{d}");
    assert!(d.rewrite.contains("mutation grantStaff"), "{d}");
    let snippet = d.rewrite.split_once("\n\nuse identity password\n").unwrap().1.split_once("\n\nMint").unwrap().0;
    let snippet_bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), format!("use identity password\n{snippet}"))]);
    assert!(snippet_bound.diagnostics.is_empty() && snippet_bound.notes.is_empty(), "{:#?} {:#?}", snippet_bound.diagnostics, snippet_bound.notes);
    let repaired = emitted_source_compiles(source, "insert <- .principal = viewer or exists staff[viewer]", &d);
    let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), repaired)]);
    assert!(bound.notes.iter().all(|n| n.code != "N_UNREADABLE_PROBE"));
}

#[test]
fn wave10_id_order_and_fanout_point_alternatives_compile_from_emitted_text() {
    let source = "table tasks:\n  score: int\n  public 'tasks'\njob rescore():\n  for row in tasks by .score:\n    update tasks[row.id] { score: 1 }\n";
    let d = refusal(source, "E_JOB_CURSOR_INDEX");
    assert!(d.rewrite.starts_with("use `for row in tasks:`"), "{d}");
    let choices: Vec<_> = d.rewrite.split('`').skip(1).step_by(2).collect();
    let repaired = source.replace("for row in tasks by .score:", choices[0]);
    assert!(compile(&[("schema.q".into(), repaired)]).diagnostics.is_empty());
    let repaired = source.replace("  score: int\n", &format!("  score: int\n  {}\n", choices[1]));
    assert!(compile(&[("schema.q".into(), repaired)]).diagnostics.is_empty());

    let source = "table reviews:\n  owner: principal\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  online only\nmutation inspect(id: reviews):\n  return count(reviews)\n";
    let d = refusal(source, "E_STRUCTURAL_FANOUT");
    let point = d.rewrite.split('`').skip(1).step_by(2).find(|part| *part == "reviews[id]").unwrap();
    let repaired = source.replace("count(reviews)", point);
    assert!(compile(&[("schema.q".into(), repaired)]).diagnostics.is_empty());
    assert!(d.rewrite.contains("maintained aggregate") && d.rewrite.contains("read rule admits"), "{d}");
}

#[test]
fn emitted_roster_recipe_preserves_an_online_rosters_extra_read_predicate() {
    let source = "table staff:\n  principal: principal\n  active: bool\n  unique principal\n  immutable principal\n  read <- .principal = viewer and .active\n  insert <- .principal = viewer or exists staff[viewer]\n  online only\ntable docs:\n  read <- exists staff[viewer]\n  sync\n";
    let d = refusal(source, "E_SYNC");
    let snippet = d.rewrite.split_once("\n\nuse identity password\n").unwrap().1.split_once("\n\nMint").unwrap().0;
    assert!(snippet.contains("  online only\n"), "{snippet}");
    assert!(snippet.contains(".principal = viewer and .active = true or viewer = 'service:ops'"), "{snippet}");
    let b = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), format!("use identity password\n{snippet}"))]);
    assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty() && b.notes.is_empty(), "{:?} {:?}", b.diagnostics, b.notes);
    emitted_source_compiles(source, "insert <- .principal = viewer or exists staff[viewer]", &d);
}

    24	/// The refusal for one comparison `cmp` in a `table`'s `effect` rule.
    25	pub(super) fn diagnostic(site: &Site, table: &str, effect: &str, transition: bool, cmp: &Expr, what: &str) -> Diagnostic {
    26	    let text = strip_parens(&crate::print::expression(cmp));

    59	pub(crate) fn named_update(table: &str, rule: &Expr, comparison: &str) -> String {

    69	/// Pasteable named form for a rule that already names one image. Bare focal

    71	pub(crate) fn named_update_source(rule: &Expr) -> String {
    72	    // @ref LLP 3000.036.002#9 — preserve authored old/next images and map bare fields to next in named transitions.

    83	pub(crate) fn transition_require(table: &str, cmp: &Expr) -> String {

    89	
    90	/// A table rule's predicate as the check a mutation states before writing:
   143	/// Rewrite each focal field `.f`, `old.f` and `next.f` through `map(image, f)`,
   144	/// leaving other member accesses alone.
   145	fn map_fields(text: &str, map: &dyn Fn(Option<&str>, &str) -> String) -> String {
   146	    let chars: Vec<char> = text.chars().collect();
   147	    let mut out = String::new();
   148	    let mut i = 0;
   149	    let name_at = |at: usize| -> String { chars[at..].iter().take_while(|c| ident(**c)).collect() };
   150	    while i < chars.len() {
   151	        let c = chars[i];
   152	        let previous = if i == 0 { None } else { Some(chars[i - 1]) };
   153	        if c == '.' && previous.is_none_or(|p| !ident(p) && p != ')' && p != ']') && chars.get(i + 1).is_some_and(|c| c.is_ascii_alphabetic()) {
   154	            let name = name_at(i + 1);
   155	            out.push_str(&map(None, &name));
   156	            i += 1 + name.chars().count();
   157	            continue;
   158	        }
   159	        if ident(c) && previous.is_none_or(|p| !ident(p) && p != '.') {
   160	            let word = name_at(i);
   161	            let after = i + word.chars().count();
   162	            if matches!(word.as_str(), "old" | "next") && chars.get(after) == Some(&'.') && chars.get(after + 1).is_some_and(|c| c.is_ascii_alphabetic()) {
   163	                let name = name_at(after + 1);
   164	                out.push_str(&map(Some(&word), &name));
   165	                i = after + 1 + name.chars().count();
   166	                continue;
   167	            }
   168	            out.push_str(&word);
   169	            i = after;
   170	            continue;
   171	        }
   172	        out.push(c);
   173	        i += 1;
   174	    }
   175	    out
   176	}
   177	
   178	fn screaming(name: &str) -> String {
   179	    let mut out = String::new();
   180	    for (i, c) in name.chars().enumerate() {
   181	        if c.is_ascii_uppercase() && i > 0 { out.push('_'); }
   182	        out.push(c.to_ascii_uppercase());
   183	    }
   184	    out
   185	}

   601	        }

   603	        if error.code == "E_QUARRY_RULE_OPERATOR" && rule.effect == Effect::Update && rule.bindings.is_empty() && error.rewrite.starts_with("name both images") {
   604	            let comparison = error.message.split('`').nth(1).unwrap_or_default().to_owned();
   605	            error.rewrite = super::rule_operator::named_update(&info.decl.name, &rule.value, &comparison);
   606	        }

   608	            error.rewrite = match rule.effect {
   609	                Effect::Read => reference::read_hop_rewrite(catalog, info, &rule.value).unwrap_or_else(|| "a read rule probes one hop: copy the deciding value onto this row (immutable, checked against the parent by the insert rule), or use read through .parent".into()),
   610	                _ => reference::reference_rewrite(catalog, info, &rule.value),
   611	            };
   612	        }
   613	        // @ref LLP 3000.036#14.3 — a read-through child binds its parent's rule over hidden
   614	        // captures (`.$enrollment.dancer`): its refusal is the parent's (`internal_names` drops the copy).
   615	        // @ref LLP 3000.036#15.6 — a `read through` that did not expand (E_READ_THROUGH says why)
   616	        // leaves its internal call; its refusal is that problem's cascade, spelled as written.

   871	fn column_ty(info: &TableInfo, name: &str) -> Option<Ty> {
   872	    if name == "id" {
   873	        return Some(Ty::Ref(info.decl.name.clone()));
   874	    }
   875	    info.columns.get(name).or_else(|| info.captures.get(name)).map(Ty::from_scalar)

   878	/// Final-insert obligations retain their exact-index write-path contract.
   879	/// Ordinary effect rules use `probe` with residual equalities enabled.
   880	pub fn bind_probe(catalog: &Catalog, info: &TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr, site: &Site, update: bool) -> Fallible<(String, String, Vec<RuleTerm>)> {
   881	    let bindings = RuleBindings { old: None, next: None, update, insert: false, hops: true, present: Vec::new() };
   882	    let (table, index, key, _) = probe(catalog, info, read, site, &bindings, false).map_err(|mut error| {
   883	        if error.code == "E_QUARRY_RULE_HOP" { error.rewrite = reference::reference_rewrite(catalog, info, &Expr::Exists { read: read.clone(), site: site.clone() }); }
   884	        error
   885	    })?;
   886	    Ok((table, index, key))
   887	}

    let update = "table fees:\n  status: enum('pending', 'confirmed')\n  public 'fees'\n  update <- old.status = 'pending' and next.status = 'confirmed'\n  online only\nmutation transition(fee: fees, status: enum('pending', 'confirmed')):\n  update fees[fee] { status }\n  return null\n";
    let diagnostic = refusal(update, "E_QUARRY_RULE_OPERATOR");
    classified(&diagnostic, SourceRewriteKind::NamedUpdate);
    assert_eq!(diagnostic.source_rewrite.as_deref(), Some("update (old, next) <- (old.status = 'pending' and next.status = 'confirmed')"));
    emitted_source_compiles(update, "update <- old.status = 'pending' and next.status = 'confirmed'", &diagnostic);

    let partially_named = "table fees:\n  owner: principal\n  status: enum('pending', 'archived')\n  public 'fees'\n  update <- .owner = viewer and old.status != 'archived'\n  online only\n";
    let diagnostic = refusal(partially_named, "E_QUARRY_RULE_OPERATOR");
    classified(&diagnostic, SourceRewriteKind::NamedUpdate);
    assert_eq!(
        diagnostic.source_rewrite.as_deref(),
        Some("update (old, next) <- ((next.owner = viewer) and (old.status != 'archived'))")
    );
    let repaired = emitted_source_compiles(
        partially_named,
        "update <- .owner = viewer and old.status != 'archived'",
        &diagnostic,
    );
    assert!(repaired.contains("update (old, next)") && repaired.contains("next.owner"));
}

    72	                    continue;

    84	                        ok = false;
    85	                    }

    90	                    }
    91	                }

   113	                            None => diagnostics.push(crate::diag::type_error(&index.site, format!("index {}.{} tokenizes unknown column {field}", table.name, index.name), name_rewrite(field, columns.keys().map(String::as_str), None))),
   114	                        }

   126	                        } else { diagnostics.push(unknown(&rule.site, format!("duplicate {} rule on {}", rule.effect.word(), table.name), "keep one rule per effect")); }

   129	                }

   133	                    // An invalid search field is a fault of that search line,
   134	                    // not evidence that every later reference names an

   402	pub fn prediction_summary(bound: &Bound) -> Option<String> {
   403	    prediction_report(bound, true)
   404	}
   405	
   406	pub fn prediction_overview(bound: &Bound) -> Option<String> {
   407	    prediction_report(bound, false)
   408	}
   409	
   410	fn prediction_report(bound: &Bound, detailed: bool) -> Option<String> {
   411	    let mut never = Vec::new();
   412	    let mut inserts = Vec::new();
   413	    let mut horizons = Vec::new();
   414	    for (name, fact) in &bound.schema.operations {
   415	        if !bound.programs.iter().any(|p| p.program.name == *name && p.program.kind == ProgramKind::Mutation) { continue; }
   416	        if fact.predicts.as_deref() == Some("never") && let Some(site) = fact.sites.iter().find(|site| site.class == SiteClass::Server) {
   417	            never.push(format!("{name} ({})", prediction_reason(&bound.schema, site)));
   418	        }
   419	        if let Some(site) = fact.sites.iter().find(|site| inserts::blocked(site)) {
   420	            inserts.push(format!("{name} ({})", site.reason));
   421	        }
   422	        let reads = horizons::reads(fact);
   423	        if fact.predicts.as_deref() == Some("eligible") && !reads.is_empty() {
   424	            horizons.push(format!("{name} ({})", reads.join(", ")));
   425	        }
   426	    }
   427	    let mut parts = Vec::new();
   428	    // @ref LLP 3000.036#12 — wave 2 (075): the summary is information, not a warning.
   429	    if !never.is_empty() { parts.push(format!("{} {} server-run (online app: no action): {}", if never.len() == 1 { "1 mutation".to_owned() } else { format!("{} mutations", never.len()) }, if never.len() == 1 { "is" } else { "are" }, never.join(", "))); }
   430	    if !inserts.is_empty() { parts.push(if detailed { format!("{} {} insert arms that never predict: {}", inserts.len(), if inserts.len() == 1 { "mutation has" } else { "mutations have" }, inserts.join(", ")) } else { format!("{} mutations have insert arms that may queue", inserts.len()) }); }
   431	    if !horizons.is_empty() { parts.push(if detailed { format!("{} {} eligible, but these reads queue without a row past a horizon: {}", horizons.len(), if horizons.len() == 1 { "mutation" } else { "mutations" }, horizons.join(", ")) } else { format!("{} eligible mutations may queue past a horizon", horizons.len()) }); }
   432	    (!parts.is_empty()).then(|| format!("{}{} — snapback4 why <op>", if bound.diagnostics.iter().any(|d| d.code == "E_SYNC") { "provisional while E_SYNC is unresolved: " } else { "" }, parts.join("; ")))
   433	}

   450	    }).collect();
   451	    if notes.len() > 1 {
   452	        let message = format!("{} synced maintains:\n{}", notes.len(), notes.iter().map(|note| format!("  {} (site {})", note.message, note.site)).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("\n"));
   453	        notes[0].message = message;
   454	        notes.truncate(1);

   456	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#8 — item 7: a readable maintain can disclose a hidden source's write delta.
   457	    for maintain in &schema.maintains {

   460	            continue;
   461	        }

   469	            if derived == target.rules.read { continue; }
   470	        }
   471	        if target.rules.read == source.rules.read && !fields.contains("id") && fields.iter().all(|field| maintain.bare_key(field)) { continue; }

   477	    }
   478	    notes
   479	}

//! Quarry text from syntax, for source-aware diagnostics and pasteable rewrites.
use crate::ast::*;

type Source<'a> = dyn Fn(&Expr) -> Option<(String, bool)> + 'a;

/// Rewrite callers may embed an expression in another operator or clause.
/// Use the same precedence renderer as source excerpts, with a grouped root.
pub fn expression(expr: &Expr) -> String {
    let grouped = match expr {
        Expr::Binary { .. } | Expr::Unary { .. } | Expr::If { .. } => true,
        Expr::Read(read) | Expr::Exists { read, .. } | Expr::Created { read, .. } =>
            read.filter.is_some() || read.matching.is_some() || read.range.is_some(),
        _ => false,
    };
    with_source_at(expr, if grouped { 8 } else { 0 }, &|_| None)
}

pub(crate) fn with_source(expr: &Expr, source: &Source<'_>) -> String { with_source_at(expr, 0, source) }
pub(crate) fn with_source_at(expr: &Expr, minimum: u8, source: &Source<'_>) -> String { render(expr, minimum, source) }

pub fn quote(value: &str) -> String {
    format!("'{}'", value.replace('\\', "\\\\").replace('\'', "\\'").replace('\n', "\\n").replace('\t', "\\t"))
}

fn priority(expr: &Expr) -> u8 {
    match expr {
        Expr::Binary { op, .. } => crate::parser::precedence(op).unwrap_or(0),
        Expr::If { .. } | Expr::Read(_) | Expr::Exists { .. } | Expr::Created { .. } | Expr::Merge(_) | Expr::Write { .. } | Expr::Walk { .. } => 0,
        Expr::Unary { .. } => 7,
        Expr::Assert { .. } | Expr::Shape { .. } | Expr::ShapeUse { .. } | Expr::Member { .. } => 8,
        _ => 9,
    }
}

fn render(expr: &Expr, minimum: u8, source: &Source<'_>) -> String {
    let wrap = |text: String, grouped: bool| if priority(expr) < minimum && !grouped { format!("({text})") } else { text };
    if let Some((text, grouped)) = source(expr) { return wrap(text, grouped); }
    let sub = |e| render(e, 0, source);
    let text = match expr {
        Expr::Name { name, .. } => name.clone(),
        Expr::Focal { .. } => ".".into(),
        Expr::Literal { value, .. } => match value {
            Literal::Str(s) => quote(s), Literal::Number(n) => n.clone(),
            Literal::Bool(b) => b.to_string(), Literal::Null => "null".into(),
        },
        Expr::Array { items, .. } => format!("[{}]", items.iter().map(sub).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")),
        Expr::Object { fields, .. } => format!("{{ {} }}", object_fields(fields, source)),
        Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } => {
            let value = render(value, 7, source);
            if op == "not" { format!("not {value}") }
            else if value.starts_with('-') { format!("-({value})") } else { format!("-{value}") }
        }
        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } => {
            let p = priority(expr);
            let (l, r) = if op == "??" { (p + 1, p) } else { (p, p + 1) };
            format!("{} {op} {}", render(left, l, source), render(right, r, source))
        }
        Expr::Member { base, name, .. } => match base.as_ref() {
            Expr::Focal { .. } => format!(".{name}"),
            other => format!("{}.{name}", render(other, 8, source)),
        },
        Expr::Call { name, args, .. } => format!("{name}({})", args.iter().enumerate().map(|(n, arg)| {
            let value = sub(arg);
            if n > 0 && matches!(name.as_str(), "day" | "hour" | "bucket") && matches!(arg, Expr::Literal { value: Literal::Number(_), .. }) && source(arg).is_none() { format!("{value}ms") }
            else { value }
        }).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")),
        Expr::Read(read) => read_with(read, source),
        Expr::Merge(merge) => format!("merge {}{}{} {} {} by {}{}:", merge.table, merge.matching.as_ref().map_or(String::new(), |q| format!(" matching {}", sub(q))), merge.cursor.as_ref().map_or(String::new(), |c| format!(" after {}", sub(c))), if merge.order == Order::Asc { "first" } else { "last" }, merge.take, merge.index, merge.filter.as_ref().map_or(String::new(), |p| format!(" where {}", sub(p)))),
        Expr::Exists { read, .. } => format!("exists {}", read_with(read, source)),
        Expr::Created { read, .. } => format!("created {}", read_with(read, source)),
        Expr::Assert { value, code, message, .. } => format!("{} ! else {code}{}", render(value, 8, source), message.as_deref().map(|m| format!(" {}", quote(m))).unwrap_or_default()),
        Expr::ShapeUse { base, name, .. } => format!("{} {name}", render(base, 8, source)),
        Expr::Shape { base, fields, .. } => format!("{} {{ {} }}", render(base, 8, source), shape_with(fields, source)),
        Expr::Walk { table, seed, column, depth, .. } => format!("walk {table}[{}] up .{column} depth {depth}", sub(seed)),
        Expr::If { then, condition, otherwise, .. } => format!("{} if {} else {}", render(then, 1, source), render(condition, 1, source), sub(otherwise)),
pub fn read_expr(read: &ReadExpr) -> String { read_with(read, &|_| None) }

fn read_with(read: &ReadExpr, source: &Source<'_>) -> String {
    if let Some((text, _)) = source(&Expr::Read(read.clone())) { return text; }
    let sub = |e| render(e, 0, source);
    if !read.order_keys.is_empty() && read.keys.iter().all(|key| key.column.is_some()) {
        let mut predicates = read.keys.iter().map(|key| format!(".{} = {}", key.column.as_ref().unwrap(), render(&key.value, 5, source))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        predicates.extend(read.seeks.iter().map(|bound| format!(".{} {} {}", bound.column, bound.op, render(&bound.value, 5, source))));
        if let Some(filter) = &read.filter { predicates.push(render(filter, if predicates.is_empty() { 0 } else { 3 }, source)); }
        let mut out = read.table.clone();
        if !predicates.is_empty() { out.push_str(&format!(" where {}", predicates.join(" and "))); }
        if let Some(matching) = &read.matching { out.push_str(&format!(" matching {}", sub(matching))); }
        if let Some(range) = &read.range { out.push_str(&format!(" {} {}", if range.order == Order::Asc { "first" } else { "last" }, range.take)); }
        out.push_str(&format!(" by {}", read.order_keys.iter().map(|key| format!(".{key}")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")));
        if let Some(cursor) = read.range.as_ref().and_then(|range| range.cursor.as_ref()) { out.push_str(&format!(" after {}", sub(cursor))); }
        return out;
    }
    let mut out = format!("{}[{}]", read.table, read.keys.iter().map(|key| key_part(key, source)).chain(read.seeks.iter().map(|b| format!("{} {} {}", b.column, b.op, sub(&b.value)))).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "));
    if let Some(matching) = &read.matching { out.push_str(&format!(" matching {}", sub(matching))); }
    if let Some(range) = &read.range {
        if let Some(cursor) = &range.cursor { out.push_str(&format!(" after {}", sub(cursor))); }
        out.push_str(&format!(" {} {}", if range.order == Order::Asc { "first" } else { "last" }, range.take));
    }
    if let Some(index) = &read.index { out.push_str(&format!(" by {index}")); }
    if let Some(filter) = &read.filter { out.push_str(&format!(" where {}", sub(filter))); }
    out
}

fn key_part(part: &KeyPart, source: &Source<'_>) -> String {
    let value = render(&part.value, 0, source);
    match &part.column { Some(column) => format!("{column} = {value}"), None => value }
}

fn object_fields(fields: &[ObjectField], source: &Source<'_>) -> String {
    fields.iter().map(|field| match &field.value {
        Some(value) if field.spread => format!("...{}", render(value, 8, source)),
        Some(value) => format!("{}: {}", field.name, render(value, 0, source)), None => field.name.clone(),
    }).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")
}

fn shape_with(fields: &[ShapeField], source: &Source<'_>) -> String {
    fields.iter().map(|field| match field {
        ShapeField::Spread { .. } => "*".to_owned(), ShapeField::NamedSpread { name, .. } => format!("...{name}"),
        ShapeField::Bare { name, .. } => name.clone(),
        ShapeField::Field { name, value, .. } => format!("{name}: {}", render(value, 0, source)),
    }).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")
}

pub fn scalar_type(type_: &ScalarType) -> String {
    let base = match &type_.kind {
        TypeKind::Cursor => "cursor".to_owned(),
        TypeKind::Text { min, max, format } => {
            let mut out = match min {
                Some(min) => format!("text {min}..{max}"),
                None => format!("text <={max}"),
            };
            if let Some(format) = format {
                out.push_str(&format!(" format {format}"));
            }
            out
        }
        TypeKind::Id => "id".into(),
        TypeKind::Principal => "principal".into(),
        TypeKind::Int => "int".into(),
        TypeKind::IntRange { min, max } => snapback4_core::schema::int_range_text(*min, *max),
        TypeKind::Decimal { scale } => format!("decimal {scale}"),
//! Verify executable repairs in the affected declaration's context.
//! Retain caller operands; never recursively verify a candidate's diagnostics.
use crate::{ast::*, bind, parser, Bound, Diagnostic, Site};
use std::ops::Range;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) enum ReadChange { NamedKey { columns: Vec<String>, index: Option<String> }, Index(String), Point, SearchOrder { keys: Vec<String>, order: Vec<String> } }

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) struct ReadRepair {
    pub site: Site,
    pub changes: Vec<ReadChange>,
}

// @ref LLP 3000.013#2.2 — index advice is source only after the patched
// declaration passes the real parser, planner and admission in its own scope.
pub(crate) fn verify_bound(documents: &[Document], diagnostics: &mut [Diagnostic]) {
    for diagnostic in diagnostics {
        let Some(repair) = diagnostic.read_repair.take() else { continue; };
        let verify = |change: &ReadChange| {
            let candidate = read_candidate(documents, &repair.site, change)?;
            (check_context(documents, &candidate, true) == Check::Accepted).then_some(candidate)
        };
        let named = repair.changes.iter().filter(|change| matches!(change, ReadChange::NamedKey { .. }))
            .filter_map(verify).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        if !named.is_empty() {
            diagnostic.message = format!("{}; choose a named form: {}", diagnostic.message.split(';').next().unwrap(), named.iter().map(|candidate| candidate.rewrite.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" or "));
        }
        let selected = named.into_iter().next().or_else(|| repair.changes.iter()
            .filter(|change| !matches!(change, ReadChange::NamedKey { .. })).find_map(verify));
        if let Some(candidate) = selected {
            if diagnostic.code == "E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN" {
                let before = &candidate.source[..candidate.replacement.start];
                let line = candidate.declaration_site.line + before.bytes().filter(|b| *b == b'\n').count() as u32;
                let col = before.rsplit('\n').next().unwrap().chars().count() as u32 + 1;
                diagnostic.verified_edit = Some((Site::new(&candidate.site.file, line, col), candidate.source[candidate.replacement].into()));
            }
            diagnostic.rewrite = candidate.rewrite;
        } else { diagnostic.rewrite.clear(); }
        if diagnostic.rewrite.is_empty() { diagnostic.mechanical = false; }
    }
}

fn read_candidate(documents: &[Document], site: &Site, change: &ReadChange) -> Option<Candidate> {
    let doc = documents.iter().find(|doc| doc.file == site.file)?;
    let (read, text, grouped, start) = doc.source.read(site)?;
    let mut fixed = read.clone();
    match change {
        ReadChange::NamedKey { columns, index } => {
            if fixed.keys.len() != columns.len() { return None; }
            for (key, column) in fixed.keys.iter_mut().zip(columns) { key.column = Some(column.clone()); }
            fixed.index = index.clone();
        }
        ReadChange::Index(index) => { fixed.index = Some(index.clone()); }
        ReadChange::SearchOrder { keys, order } => {
            if fixed.matching.is_none() || fixed.keys.len() > keys.len() { return None; }
            for (key, column) in fixed.keys.iter_mut().zip(keys) { key.column.get_or_insert_with(|| column.clone()); }
            fixed.index = None;
            fixed.order_keys = order.clone();
        }
        ReadChange::Point => {
            // A point cannot silently discard a population predicate or cursor.
            if read.filter.is_some() || read.matching.is_some() || !read.seeks.is_empty()
                || !read.order_keys.is_empty() || read.range.as_ref()?.cursor.is_some() { return None; }
            fixed.range = None;
        }
    }
    // Retain the caller's operands, including grouping, literals and comments.
    // Never invent expressions from the selected index's column names.
    let fixed = Expr::Read(fixed);
    let rewrite = crate::print::with_source_at(&fixed, if grouped { 8 } else { 0 }, &|expr| doc.source.get(expr));
    let (declaration_site, source, replacement) = doc.source.declarations.iter().find_map(|(key, source)| {
        let (key, col) = key.rsplit_once(':')?;
        let (file, line) = key.rsplit_once(':')?;
        let declaration_site = Site::new(file, line.parse().ok()?, col.parse().ok()?);
        if file != site.file { return None; }
        let line = start.line.checked_sub(declaration_site.line)? as usize;
        let lines = source.split('\n').collect::<Vec<_>>();
        let column = start.col.checked_sub(1)? as usize;
        let byte = lines.get(line)?.char_indices().map(|(i, _)| i).chain([lines[line].len()]).nth(column)?;
        let offset = lines[..line].iter().map(|s| s.len() + 1).sum::<usize>() + byte;
        let span = offset..offset.checked_add(text.len())?;
        (source.get(span.clone())? == text.as_str()).then_some((declaration_site, source.clone(), span))
    })?;
    Some(Candidate { site: site.clone(), declaration_site, source, replacement, rewrite, intended: None, message: None })
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) struct Candidate {
    pub site: Site,
    pub declaration_site: Site,
    pub source: String,
    pub replacement: Range<usize>,
    pub rewrite: String,
    pub intended: Option<ReadExpr>,
    pub message: Option<String>,
}

pub(crate) fn verify(documents: &[Document], diagnostics: &mut [Diagnostic]) {
    for diagnostic in diagnostics {
        // Other diagnostics include design advice rather than executable edits.
        // These are the concrete repairs introduced by the familiarity lane.
        if diagnostic.code != "E_QUARRY_SELF_EQUALITY"
            && !(diagnostic.code == "E_QUARRY_UNSUPPORTED" && diagnostic.message.starts_with("count(*)")) { continue; }
        if diagnostic.rewrite.is_empty() { continue; }
        let candidate = documents.iter().flat_map(|doc| &doc.source.repairs).rev()
            .find(|candidate| candidate.site == diagnostic.site && candidate.rewrite == diagnostic.rewrite);
        let verified = candidate.and_then(|candidate| {
            // A where population cannot become a nullable unique point, even
            // when the bare point compiles (for example, a required outer key).
            if nullable_key(documents, candidate) && let Some(page) = key_page(documents, candidate) {
                return (check(documents, &page) == Check::Accepted).then_some(page);
            }
            match check(documents, candidate) {
                Check::Accepted => Some(candidate.clone()),
                Check::Unbound => {
                    let page = key_page(documents, candidate)?;
                    (check(documents, &page) == Check::Accepted).then_some(page)
                }
                Check::Rejected => None,
            }
        });
        if let Some(candidate) = verified {
            diagnostic.rewrite = candidate.rewrite;
            if let Some(message) = candidate.message { diagnostic.message = message; }
        } else { diagnostic.rewrite.clear(); }

   762	            Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } if op == "not" => absence(value, !inverted),
   763	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "=" || op == "!=" => {

   765	                    (value, Expr::Literal { value: Literal::Bool(b), .. }) | (Expr::Literal { value: Literal::Bool(b), .. }, value) => Some((value, *b)),
   766	                    _ => None,
   767	                };

   769	            }
   770	            Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => absence(left, inverted).into_iter().chain(absence(right, inverted)).collect(),
   771	            Expr::If { then, condition, otherwise, .. } => {
   772	                let mut all = Vec::new(); let mut copy = (**condition).clone();
   773	                super::intent_walk::expression(&mut copy, &mut |expr| { if let Expr::Exists { read, .. } = expr { all.push(read.clone()); } });
   774	                all.extend(absence(then, inverted)); all.extend(absence(otherwise, inverted)); all

//! Rule evaluation and the static facts about it that the engine hoists: a
//! rule that is proven true by the scanned prefix (`decided_true`) costs no
//! probes; a rule whose deciding fields are pinned by the prefix (`uniform`) is
//! decided once for the whole range. Copied from Snapback 2's uniformity
//! analysis (LLP 2000.000 §5.1); the certificate that priced these is gone,
//! the analysis is not.

use crate::ir::{Bound, Expr, IndexKeyExpr, Input, Merge, Scan};
use crate::schema::{ColumnType, RuleExpr, RuleTerm, Schema, Table};
use crate::value::Value;
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Visibility {
    /// One verdict serves every row of the scanned prefix.
    pub uniform: bool,
    /// That verdict is `true` before any row is read.
    pub decided_true: bool,
}

pub fn scan_visibility(schema: &Schema, scan: &Scan) -> Visibility {
    let Some(table) = schema.table(&scan.table) else { return Visibility::default() };
    let Some(index) = table.index(&scan.index) else { return Visibility::default() };
    let pinned = |name: &str| index.components.iter().zip(&scan.prefix).find_map(|(part, key)| (part.column().is_some_and(|column| column == name)).then(|| key.scalar()).flatten());
    visibility(&scan.table, table, &table.rules.read, &pinned)

}

// @ref LLP 3000.007#20.5 — a missing upsert may insert only when the complete held group and its key decide that no hidden target can exist.
pub fn upsert_absence(schema: &Schema, name: &str, index: &str, key: &[Expr]) -> Result<Visibility, &'static str> {
    let table = schema.table(name).ok_or("the target is online only")?;
    let sync = table.sync.as_ref().ok_or("the target is online only")?;
    if sync.horizon.is_some() { return Err("the target has a horizon"); }
    let rule = table.rules.read.device_audience_rule();
    // Public subset sync has no whole-table absence proof. Its group coverage
    // is a separate decision; do not infer it merely from the public read rule.
    if rule.is_uniformly_readable() && sync.audience != crate::schema::Audience::Public { return Err("the key's synced group is not established"); }
    let index = table.index(index).ok_or("the key does not pin readability")?;
    let pinned = crate::small_map::collect(index.components.iter().zip(key).filter_map(|(part, expression)| part.column().map(|column| (column, expression))));
    let visibility = prefix_visibility(name, table, &rule, &pinned);
    if !visibility.uniform { return Err("the key does not pin readability"); }
    Ok(visibility)
}

// @ref LLP 3000.007#18.1 — simplify self-witnesses, then pre-check only the leading conjuncts whose fields the scope pins, in evaluation order.
pub(super) fn prefix_decision(schema: &Schema, scan: &Scan) -> Option<RuleExpr> {
    let table = schema.table(&scan.table)?;
    let index = table.index(&scan.index)?;
    let pinned: BTreeMap<_,_> = crate::small_map::collect(index.components.iter().zip(&scan.prefix).filter_map(|(part, key)| part.column().zip(key.scalar())));
    if table.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|t| pinned.contains_key(&t.reference)) { return Some(table.rules.read.clone()); }
    let rule = simplify_prefix(&table.rules.read, &scan.table, table, &pinned);
    if matches!(rule, RuleExpr::Allow) { return None; }
    let mut terms = Vec::new();
    conjuncts(rule, &mut terms);
    let decided: Vec<_> = terms.into_iter().take_while(|term| {
        let mut fields = BTreeSet::new();
        term.fields(&mut fields);
        fields.iter().all(|field| pinned.contains_key(field))
    }).collect();
    (!decided.is_empty()).then_some(RuleExpr::And(decided))
}

// Flatten only conjunctions, preserving left-to-right short-circuit order.
fn conjuncts(rule: RuleExpr, out: &mut Vec<RuleExpr>) {
    match rule { RuleExpr::And(terms) => for term in terms { conjuncts(term, out); }, rule => out.push(rule) }
}

fn simplify_prefix(rule: &RuleExpr, name: &str, table: &Table, pinned: &BTreeMap<&String, &Expr>) -> RuleExpr {
    let pin = |name: &str| pinned.iter().find_map(|(key, value)| (key.as_str() == name).then_some(*value));
    if rule_known(rule, true, name, table, &pin) { return RuleExpr::Allow; }
    if rule_known(rule, false, name, table, &pin) { return RuleExpr::Deny; }
    match rule {
        RuleExpr::And(terms) => {
            let mut out = Vec::new();
            for term in terms { conjuncts(simplify_prefix(term, name, table, pinned), &mut out); }
            out.retain(|term| !matches!(term, RuleExpr::Allow));
            RuleExpr::And(out)
        },
        RuleExpr::Or(_) => {
            // Use the same self-witness absorption as uniformity, but remove
            // the absorbed field read from the expression we actually run.
            let terms = rule.disjuncts();
            let mut out: Vec<_> = terms.iter()
                .filter(|term| !terms.iter().any(|other| self_probe_absorbs(name, table, term, other)))
                .map(|term| simplify_prefix(term, name, table, pinned))
                .filter(|term| !matches!(term, RuleExpr::Deny))
                .collect();
            match out.len() {
                0 => RuleExpr::Deny,
                1 => out.pop().unwrap(),
                _ => RuleExpr::Or(out),
            }
        }
        RuleExpr::Not(inner) => {
            let inner = simplify_prefix(inner, name, table, pinned);
            negate_required_bool(&inner, table).unwrap_or_else(|| RuleExpr::Not(Box::new(inner)))
        }
        RuleExpr::Ne(left, right) => required_bool_equality(left, right, table, false).unwrap_or_else(|| rule.clone()),
        _ => rule.clone(),
    }
}

/// Required booleans have exactly two values, so their negated equality and
/// inequality forms can share one positive equality identity. Optional bools
/// retain their original expression because `null` is distinct from `false`.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#9 — prefix simplification and guard coverage use the same required-Boolean identity.
fn required_bool_equality(left: &RuleTerm, right: &RuleTerm, table: &Table, equal: bool) -> Option<RuleExpr> {
    [(left, right), (right, left)].into_iter().find_map(|(field, value)| {
        let (RuleTerm::Field(column), RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Bool(expected))) = (field, value) else { return None };
        matches!(table.columns.get(column), Some(ColumnType::Bool)).then(|| RuleExpr::Eq(
            RuleTerm::Field(column.clone()),
            RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Bool(if equal { *expected } else { !*expected })),
        ))
    })
}

fn negate_required_bool(rule: &RuleExpr, table: &Table) -> Option<RuleExpr> {
    match rule {
        RuleExpr::Allow => Some(RuleExpr::Deny),
        RuleExpr::Deny => Some(RuleExpr::Allow),
        RuleExpr::Eq(left, right) => required_bool_equality(left, right, table, false),
        RuleExpr::Ne(left, right) => required_bool_equality(left, right, table, true),
        _ => None,
    }
}

/// A read rule `A or G` whose disjunct `G` reads no column of the row (a
/// role such as `exists staff[viewer]`, or a service seat) is true for every
/// row whenever `G` holds for this caller. The page is then decided once, per
                out.entry(binding.clone()).or_default().writes += 1;
                collect(body, false, out);
            }
            Stmt::If {
                then_body,
                else_body,
                ..
            } => {
                collect(then_body, false, out);
                collect(else_body, false, out);
            }
            _ => {}
        }
    }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.025#6-diagnostics-and-exact-documentation-changes — name the enclosing statement, not an inferred product invariant.
fn expiry_problem(program: &Program, scan: &Scan, boundary: std::ops::Range<usize>, never: bool, in_prefix: bool) -> Problem {
    let mut message = format!("{} scans expiring {}", program.name, scan.table);
    let mut rewrite = if never { String::new() } else { format!("Make `{}` `: server`. ", program.name) };
    if in_prefix {
        message.push_str(" but may predict");
    } else if let Some(statement) = program.body[boundary.clone()].last() {
        let group = &program.body[boundary.clone()];
        let (inside, _) = crate::ir::body_read_structure(group);
        let enclosing = inside.iter().any(|candidate| std::ptr::eq(*candidate, scan));
        let site = match statement {
            Stmt::If { .. } => "if".into(),
            Stmt::For { .. } => "for".into(),
            Stmt::Emit { effect, .. } => format!("emit {effect}"),
            Stmt::Const { name, .. } | Stmt::Let { name, .. } | Stmt::Assign { name, .. } if !name.starts_with('$') =>
            {
                format!("binding {name}")
            }
            Stmt::Update { table, .. } => format!("update {table}"),
            Stmt::Upsert { table, .. } => format!("upsert {table}"),
            Stmt::WriteTarget { target, upsert, .. } => format!("{} {}", if *upsert { "upsert" } else { "update" }, target.table),
            Stmt::Delete { table, .. } => format!("delete {table}"),
            Stmt::DeleteTarget { target } => format!("delete {}", target.table),
            Stmt::Insert { table, .. } | Stmt::InsertIfAbsent { table, .. } => {
                format!("insert {table}")
            }
            _ => format!("statement {}", boundary.start + 1),
        };
        let effect = if super::read_only::statements::<true>(group) { "an emit" } else { "a write" };
        message.push_str(&if enclosing {
            format!(" inside `{site}` containing {effect} (an untaken arm counts)")
        } else {
            format!(" after `{site}` containing {effect}, ending the read-only phase")
        });
        if enclosing && let Stmt::For { .. } = statement {
            let single = Program { body: vec![statement.clone()], ..program.clone() };
            let writes = crate::ir::write_tables(&single);
            let action = writes.first().map_or("…", |(_, kind)| *kind);
            rewrite.push_str(&format!("Bind before this entire loop: `page = {} …`; `for row in page: {action} …`.", scan.table));
        } else {
            rewrite.push_str(&format!("Move this scan into its own statement above the entire `{site}`. Then write."));
        }
    }
    Problem { code: "E_STRUCTURAL_FANOUT", site: scan.site.clone(), page_site: Some(scan.site.clone()), message, rewrite: rewrite.trim().into() }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.025#3-proposed-rule — expiry alone may pass the server read-only prefix; matching and merges keep their refusal.
fn plain_prefix_problem(schema: &Schema, scan: &Scan) -> Option<String> {
    let table = schema.table(&scan.table)?;
    if !table.index(&scan.index)?.is_plain() || scan.prefix.iter().any(|key| matches!(key, IndexKeyExpr::WordPrefix(_))) {
        Some("matching word scans expand hidden words before deciding visibility; read it in a query, or use a plain prefix scan".into())
    } else if scan.merge_site.is_some() {
        Some("a merge is not a plain prefix scan; read it in a query, or use a job chunk".into())
    } else { None }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.007#14 — ruling 17 (c): binding a page has the same abort channel as looping over it.
fn require_uniform_mutation_pages(schema: &Schema, program: &Program, scans: &[&Scan]) -> Result<(), Problem> {
    for scan in scans {
        if schema.table(&scan.table).and_then(|table| table.index(&scan.index)).is_some() && !decided(schema, program, scan) {
            return Err(fanout(Some(scan), &scan.site, "the mutation page's read rule is not uniformly decided by its prefix", &uniform_rewrite(schema, scan)));
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

/// The page's verdict is one for every row: its prefix decides the read rule,
/// or a dominating `require` of a readable grant does (`rules::grant_dominated`).
// @ref LLP 3000.036#12 — a staff-only pass after `require exists staff[viewer]` reads every row it walks.
fn decided(schema: &Schema, program: &Program, scan: &Scan) -> bool {
    scan_visibility(schema, scan).uniform || crate::engine::rules::grant_dominated(schema, program, scan)
}

fn fanout(scan: Option<&Scan>, site: &str, reason: &str, rewrite: &str) -> Problem {
    let reason = reason.split_once("; ").map_or(reason, |(reason, _)| reason);
    let population = scan.map(|scan| format!("{} by {} (page bound {}): ", scan.table, scan.index, scan.take.maximum())).unwrap_or_default();
    Problem { code: "E_STRUCTURAL_FANOUT", site: site.into(), page_site: scan.map(|scan| scan.site.clone()), message: format!("{population}{reason}"), rewrite: format!("{rewrite}; for a single row use a point read by id (`table[id]`); for a global count/total read a maintained aggregate whose read rule explicitly admits the caller (public only when its totals may be public)") }
}

    fn body(catalog: &Catalog, schema: &Schema, program: &str, statements: &[Stmt], mut known: BTreeSet<String>, global: &BTreeSet<String>, guard_names: &BTreeSet<String>, system: bool, observe_prefix: bool, visiting: &mut BTreeSet<String>, notes: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) -> BTreeSet<String> {
